- **api-type-contract-review**: Reviews a diff for API/contract violations, type-safety failures, and structural/architectural boundary violations — breaking signature changes, incompatible request/response schema changes, unsafe type widenings/casts, contract mismatches across language/service boundaries, newly introduced circular dependencies, layering violations, and encapsulation bypasses. Invoke when triage routes to api-type-contract-review, or directly when a diff changes a public function/endpoint signature, exported type, DTO/schema, or adds a new import/dependency edge between modules. - **app-investigator**: Read-only investigator for the application layer of an incident — deployments, recent releases and syncs, configuration and Helm values, pod health, error categories in logs. Given a case file, it returns competing application-layer hypotheses with evidence for and against, citing each query. Use it from the incident-triage skill, in parallel with network-investigator and db-investigator. It never changes anything and never proposes fixes. ([Octoco-AI/ai-course-exercises](https://github.com/Octoco-AI/ai-course-exercises/blob/HEAD/combo8-agentic-devops/ops-starter-kit/.claude/agents/app-investigator.md))
- **app-lifecycle-governor**: App lifecycle governance: ownership audit with orphan detection, SLA validation, data classification checks, lifecycle-stage tracking (prototype → staging → production → deprecated → archived), and deprecation-plan verification. Read-only — findings are recommendations, not mandates. ([Popoboxxo/ha-command-gauge](https://github.com/Popoboxxo/ha-command-gauge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-lifecycle-governor.md) · 2 copies)
- **app-listing-copywriter**: Use for honest Shopify App Store listing drafts, screenshots, support and legal links. ([khadinakbarlabs/shopify-app-builder](https://github.com/khadinakbarlabs/shopify-app-builder/blob/HEAD/agents/app-listing-copywriter.md) · ★3)
- **app-locator**: Mechanical app locator for exact files, symbols, usages, declarations, and tests without analysis ([bernhardberger/tvheadend-player](https://github.com/bernhardberger/tvheadend-player/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/app-locator.md) · ★16)
- **app-log**: Read this before diagnosing a report about the running app. `Handpic.log` is the only trace a session leaves: stderr is not captured in a bundled app, so anything that matters is written here, and the failures an agent most needs carry a fixed, greppable prefix (`panic:`, `invariant:`). ([minodisk/handpic](https://github.com/minodisk/handpic/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/app-log.md))
- **app-mapper**: Use this agent when the user asks to "understand the application", "map the codebase", "analyze the architecture", "identify trust boundaries", "map user roles", or needs to build comprehensive application understanding before vulnerability hunting. ([allsmog/vuln-scout](https://github.com/allsmog/vuln-scout/blob/HEAD/vuln-scout/agents/app-mapper.md) · ★26)
- **app-mapper**: Reads the host frontend repo's source to map routes to source files and components, and reports which pages already have usable test ids. Writes .pi/app-map.md. Never modifies app source. ([vlangman/speed-run](https://github.com/vlangman/speed-run/blob/HEAD/agents/app-mapper.md))
- **app-models**: Node-only public entry: `@happyvertical/smrt-core/app-models`. `await createAppModelEditor(options)` is the single programmatic editor. The shared [app tools](app-tools.md) adapt human labels for MCP/assistant use. Await construction before serving: it replays persisted definitions and completes any pending activation. ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/core/agents/app-models.md) · ★7)
- **app-modernization**: Advises Project Khepri plans with proven application modernization patterns, selection criteria, risks, and regression checks. ([Tyler-R-Kendrick/project-khepri](https://github.com/Tyler-R-Kendrick/project-khepri/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/app-modernization.md) · ★2)
- **app-modernizer**: Specializes in application modernization and creates phase-dedicated Squads for app modernization increments. ([Tyler-R-Kendrick/project-khepri](https://github.com/Tyler-R-Kendrick/project-khepri/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/app-modernizer.agent.md) · ★2)
- **app-motion**: Phase 8 of app-craft. Adds motion that is feedback and continuity — never spectacle. Hover/focus ≤120ms, nothing over 300ms, transform/opacity only, optimistic UI, shared-element continuity, direction-bearing enter/exit, pending states that don't reflow. Explicitly bans landing-page motion (scroll-jacking, smooth-scroll libraries, count-ups, parallax) from application UI. Reads the built app; applies app-craft/references/app-motion.md. ([propiter/landing-craft](https://github.com/propiter/landing-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/app-motion.md) · ★1)
- **app-movil**: Implementa pantallas y logica de la app Expo consumiendo la API. Usar cuando el contrato de la API ya este en la especificacion; no toca la API. ([Tuarez2/barberly](https://github.com/Tuarez2/barberly/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-movil.md))
- **app-notes**: Read this before changing Petal Notes, helpers in notes or Petal's viewer; then read the part of `docs/petal-notes.md` you are about to change. ([edgetr/petal](https://github.com/edgetr/petal/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/app-notes.md) · ★2)
- **app-onboarding**: Guide activation with server-derived progress and accessible Svelte checklists. ([legioncodeinc/develop-like-a-girl-advanced-resources](https://github.com/legioncodeinc/develop-like-a-girl-advanced-resources/blob/HEAD/plugins/dlag-advanced-app-builder/agents/app-onboarding.md))
- **app-onboarding**: Add a simple accessible Starter onboarding checklist tied to verified application state. ([legioncodeinc/develop-like-a-girl-starter-resources](https://github.com/legioncodeinc/develop-like-a-girl-starter-resources/blob/HEAD/plugins/dlag-starter-app-builder/agents/app-onboarding.md))
- **app-orchestrator**: Webアプリの準備→要件定義→設計→実装→公開→品質ゲートを実行する内部オーケストレーター。Claude Codeの /build-app /improve-app、Codexの $build-app / $improve-app から明示的に委譲された場合、custom agent起動時、または利用者が $app-orchestrator を明示した場合だけ使用する。一般のアプリ相談から暗黙起動しない。 ([daishiman/harness-dev](https://github.com/daishiman/harness-dev/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-orchestrator.md) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **app-orchestrator**: Chef d'orchestre de FBMS. Coordonne les agents, distribue les tâches, consolide les résultats et empêche deux agents d'écrire dans le même fichier. ([nwodobe/fbms](https://github.com/nwodobe/fbms/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-orchestrator.md))
- **app-owner**: Owns the composition root and the store — src/app/, src/store/, src/main.tsx. Use for the profile switch, RTK Query registration, slices, typed hooks, or wiring a feature into the app. Never edits src/shared/ or the inside of a feature. ([marinvch/weather-app](https://github.com/marinvch/weather-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-owner.md))
- **app-packaging**: Default to a native NixOS module (`services.&lt;app&gt;`) when nixpkgs has one. Containers are the fallback, not the baseline: they add a podman layer, separate volume bookkeeping under `/var/lib/containers/&lt;app&gt;`, and inter-app DNS that native services don't need. Either way, [architecture.md](architecture.md) governs the module's shape. ([ianepreston/nixos](https://github.com/ianepreston/nixos/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/app-packaging.md) · ★1)
- **app-performance-engineer**: Use when you need to profile and improve application performance safely — frontend budgets and Core Web Vitals, backend latency and flame graphs, caching, and memory or resource leaks. This agent measures first, then makes focused, verified changes. ([FluxonLab/Skillry](https://github.com/FluxonLab/Skillry/blob/HEAD/plugins/performance-and-cost/agents/app-performance-engineer.md) · ★10)
- **app-persona**: Plays ONE named persona of this product, using the real app in character, and reports friction as that person would feel it during a working day. Use one agent per persona so their verdicts stay independent. Judges trust, copy and fit to the job, not only whether buttons work. Reads the personas from the product's domain skill and does nothing until they are written. Does not edit files. ([ajaynarang/app-foundation](https://github.com/ajaynarang/app-foundation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-persona.md))
- **app-planner**: Optional read-only planning second opinion for one coherent Android TV architecture or implementation outcome ([bernhardberger/tvheadend-player](https://github.com/bernhardberger/tvheadend-player/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/app-planner.md) · ★16)
- **app-planner**: Guide users from app idea to feature breakdown to implementation plan. - **app-polish**: Phase 9 of app-craft — the craft pass. Tightens density, alignment, typography and spacing; builds the keyboard model (roving tabindex, shortcuts, focus management, ⌘K); verifies ARIA patterns; measures WCAG AA contrast in BOTH themes; and fixes responsive behaviour down to 320px and 200% zoom. Reads the built app; applies Impeccable + interaction-a11y + the contrast gate. ([propiter/landing-craft](https://github.com/propiter/landing-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/app-polish.md) · ★1)
- **app-privacy-compliance**: Reviews the privacy regulation and store-policy obligations that gate shipping, as engineering facts, not legal conclusions. Covers consent ordering, data minimization, deletion propagation, third-party SDKs as processors, and Privacy Manifests / ATT / Data Safety forms. Distinct from `security`, `web-analytics`, `platform-release`. ([austintheriot/dotfiles](https://github.com/austintheriot/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-privacy-compliance.md) · ★1)
- **app-product**: Phase 1 of app-craft — establishes WHAT the app does, WHO uses it (roles), the jobs-to-be-done ranked by frequency, and a teardown of the reference-class UIs in this category. ([propiter/landing-craft](https://github.com/propiter/landing-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/app-product.md) · ★1)
- **app-prompt-engineer**: Compiles a settled app brief into a build-ready spec, or audits a spec for gaps. Dispatch after the interview — the AskUserQuestion interview stays in the main thread. - **app-prototyper**: Patterson internal-tools prototyper. - **app-release-manager**: Use this agent when preparing to release a new version of MirrorBuddy. - **app-review**: Phase 10 (the gate) of app-craft. - **approval-judge**: Escalation gate for {{PROJECT}}. Given a proposed or in-progress change, it renders a single decision, PROCEED or APPROVAL REQUIRED, based on whether the change materially alters the product vision, the scope, or a contract the maintainer must sign off on. Deliberately high threshold: it defaults to PROCEED and stops work only for genuine vision or contract-level changes. It also reports posture drift between the project's declaration sites and its recorded intent. It judges only; it does not design, implement, or write code. ([ev4sec/roll-call-engine](https://github.com/ev4sec/roll-call-engine/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/approval-judge.md))
